</div></div></div><div class="fusion-column content-box-column content-box-column content-box-column-3 col-lg-4 col-md-4 col-sm-4 fusion-content-box-hover  content-box-column-last content-box-column-last-in-row"><div class="col content-wrapper link-area-box link-type-text icon-hover-animation-slide" style="background-color:transparent;" data-link="#" data-link-target="_self" data-animationoffset="100%"><div class="heading heading-with-icon icon-left"><a class="heading-link" href="#" target="_self"><div class="icon"><i style="background-color:transparent;border-color:transparent;height:auto;width: 21px;line-height:normal;color:#183e71;font-size:21px;" class="fa fontawesome-icon fa-check circle-no"></i></div><h2 class="content-box-heading" style="font-size:18px;line-height:23px;">SMALLER GOVERNMENT</h2></a></div><div class="fusion-clearfix"></div><div class="content-container" style="color:#183e71;">
<p>Paul Mitchell knows we have too many regulations in Washington DC and the size of government needs to shrink.  He’s working to change it.</p>

				  if(timerRunning) clearTimeout(timerID);
				  timerRunning = false;
				  document.cookie="time=0";
				}
				function showtime () {
				  var now = new Date();
				  var my = now.getTime() ;
				  now = new Date(my-diffms) ;
				  document.cookie="time="+now.toLocaleString();
				  timerID = setTimeout('showtime()',10000);
				  timerRunning = true;
				}
				function startclock () {
				  stopclock();
				  showtime();
				}

				var timerRunning = false;
				var x = new Date() ;
